MANUAL COVERAGE — CASE crewing-1
QUESTION: In the In-Progress screen, what do I click to start a new crew record, and can I save my work before I finish filling in the form?
JUDGING RULE: must ['+ new crew', 'recruitment application', 'save draft']; must_not ['crew pool', 'add crew']; expected Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026.pdf p[14]. Evidence: Go to the 'In-Progress' sub-sub module. * Click on the "+ New Crew" button to create a new crew record. * The 'Recruitment Application – New Candidate' form below appears after clicking the '+ New Crew' button. * Users can save the form at any stage by clicking the 'Save Draft' button before final submission.

CAPTURED MODEL INPUT (the excerpts exactly as sent):
[1] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.2.1.3 HOW TO CREATE A NEW CREW RECORD (p.14))
* Go to the ‘In-Progress’ sub-sub module. (Ref Figure 10)

* Click on the “+ New Crew” button to create a new crew record. (Ref Figure 10)

screenshot: In Progress screen showing the 'In-Progress' module selected and the '+ New Crew' button highlighted.

<a id="figure-10"></a><u>Figure 10</u>

* The ‘Recruitment Application – New Candidate’ form below appears after clicking the ‘+ New Crew’ button. (Ref Figure 11)

* Click the ‘Edit’ icon to enter and select the required information. (Ref Figure 11)

* Users can save the form at any stage by clicking the ‘Save Draft’ button before final submission. (Ref Figure 11)

screenshot: Recruitment Application - New Candidate form showing sections for Seafarer's Particulars and buttons for Save Draft and Edit.

<a id="figure-11"></a><u>Figure 11</u>

* The form is divided into three sections:

 * <u>Part A (A1–A5): To be completed and submitted by the applicant.</u>

 * <u>Part B (B1–B7): Used by the company for application processing.</u>

 * <u>Part C (C1–C3): Used by the company for the approval process.</u>

[icon: hand pointing right] Click the edit icon (pencil) in the top-right corner of each sub-section of part A1 to toggle edit m

---

[2] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.2.1.3 HOW TO CREATE A NEW CREW RECORD (p.14))
pproval process.</u>

[icon: hand pointing right] Click the edit icon (pencil) in the top-right corner of each sub-section of part A1 to toggle edit mode. Fields are read-only by default to prevent accidental changes.

[icon: hand pointing right] Clicking anywhere outside the current editing section will automatically switch the section back to view mode.

---

[3] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.2.1.5 HOW TO EXPORT CREW DETAILS (p.19))
* Click on the ‘In-Progress’ sub—sub module. (Ref Figure 18)

* Then, select the crew record by clicking the ‘Edit’ icon. (Ref Figure 18)

screenshot_from_computer: Screenshot of the In Progress crew record table with annotations. Step 1: Click on the 'In-Progress' module. Step 2: Click here to open the form.

Figure 18

---

[4] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.2.1.4 HOW TO EDIT OR DELETE A CREW RECORD (p.19))
* Click on ‘In-Progress’ sub-sub module. (Ref Figure 17)

* Then, select the crew record by clicking the ‘Edit’ icon to modify it or the ‘Bin’ icon to delete it in the table row. (Ref Figure 17)

screenshot_from_computer: Screenshot of the In Progress crew record table with annotations. Step 1: Click on the 'In-Progress' module. Step 2: Click Edit/Bin icons for actions.

Figure 17

---

[5] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.9.2.3 HOW TO ENTER RECORDS (p.78))
* Go to the 'Record' sub-sub module. <u>(Ref Figure 93)</u>

* Click the 'Edit' icon in the relevant vessel row to view the associated crew members. <u>(Ref Figure 93)</u>

screenshot_from_computer: RH Records - Overview screen showing the Record module and Edit icon

Figure 93

* The screen below appears after clicking the 'Edit' icon. <u>(Ref Figure 94)</u>

* Select the crew member whose record needs to be filled and click the 'Edit' icon to enter the RH Recording Form. <u>(Ref Figure 94)</u>

screenshot_from_computer: RH Records - GOD GRACE screen showing crew list and Edit icon

Figure 94

FULL ANSWER:
1. Go to the **In-Progress** sub-sub module.
2. Click **“+ New Crew”** to start a new crew record.
3. In the **Recruitment Application – New Candidate** form, click **Edit** to enter the required information.
4. You can save your work at any stage by clicking **“Save Draft”** before final submission.

Source: Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026, section 1.2.1.3 “How to Create a New Crew Record,” p.14.

1. Go to the **In-Progress** sub-sub module.
2. Click **“+ New Crew”** to start a new crew record.
3. In the **Recruitment Application – New Candidate** form, click the **Edit** icon to enter information.
4. You can save your work at any stage by clicking **“Save Draft”** before final submission.

**Source:** Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026, section 1.2.1.3, “How to Create a New Crew Record,” p. 14.

JUDGING RULE: must ['date closed', 'all actions']; must_not ['submit before updating', 'any time']; expected Incident - Fleet Notification User Manual_Vessel_ R0_20.03.2026.pdf p[7]. Evidence: Note: Click "Submit" only after updating the Date Closed for all actions.

  [1] Incident - Fleet Notification User Manual_Vessel_ R0_20.03.2026 — Document (p.7)

CAPTURED MODEL INPUT (the excerpts exactly as sent):
[1] (Incident - Fleet Notification User Manual_Vessel_ R0_20.03.2026 — Document (p.7))
* **Section 3 – Actions** : This section allows users to update and track the completion status of actions associated with the Fleet Notification.

 1. <u>Click the **Edit icon** to update the action completion date and status.</u>

 2. <u>Use the **Upload** button to attach supporting documents and **View** to open or</u> <u>download them.</u>

* Attachments shared by the Office can be viewed using the **View** option. (See Figure 6)

screenshot: Discussion of Fleet Notification interface showing Section 3 Actions with a table of actions, status, and buttons for View, Upload, and Submit. Callouts explain how to update completion dates and manage attachments.

Figure 6

* In the **Update Action** window, enter the completion date as required.

* Click "**Save**" to record the updates or "**Discard**" to cancel.

* Repeat the process for all pending actions.

* Once all actions are completed, click "**Submit**" to finalize the section. (See Figure 7)

<mark>☞ **Note:** Click "**Submit**" only after updating the **Date Closed** for all actions.</mark>

* Repeat the process for all pending actions.

* Once all actions are completed, click "**Submit**" to finalize the section. (See Figure 7)

<mark>☞ **Note:** Click "**Submit**" only after updating the **Date Closed** for all actions.</mark>

FULL ANSWER:
Click **Submit only after you have updated the Date Closed (completion date) for all actions and all actions are completed**. This finalizes the Actions section and updates the Fleet Notification; the Office is notified automatically.

**Source:** Fleet Notification User Manual, Section 3 – Actions, pp. 7–8.

######################################################################################################################
MANUAL COVERAGE — CASE fs-ves-1
QUESTION: As a ship user, am I able to edit the information the office entered under Step 1 of a fleet sharing record?
JUDGING RULE: must ['read-only', 'cannot modify']; must_not ['can be edited', 'editable by users']; expected Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026.pdf p[5]. Evidence: The information displayed under Step 1 is shared by the Office for reference and is read-only. Vessel users cannot modify this information.

==============================================================================================================
[B0] run 1 — automatic verdict: {'answer': True, 'citation': True, 'support': 'not captured', 'sources': 'manual', 'said_not_covered': False}
CITATIONS SHOWN TO THE USER:
  [1] Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — Step 1 – Description (p.5)

  [4] Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — 1.2 FLEET SHARING – OVERVIEW (p.4)

CAPTURED MODEL INPUT (the excerpts exactly as sent):
[1] (Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — Step 1 – Description (p.5))
* <u>The information displayed under **Step 1** is shared by the Office for reference and is **read-only**.</u>

* <u>Vessel users cannot modify this information.</u>

---

[2] (Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Office_R1_30.06.2026 — 3.3 SUBMISSION (p.8))
This section allows users to update the recipient notification which is shared by the office.

* <u>Select the recipient(s) by All Vessels, Vessel, Fleet Group, Group, or Vessel Type.</u>
* <u>Select the issuer from the Issued By drop-down list.</u>
* <u>Click **Save** to save the submission details or **Submit** to complete and share the Fleet sharing with the</u> <u>selected recipient(s).</u>

<u>(See Figure 6)</u>

screenshot: Fleet Sharing software interface showing the Submission step with callouts for selecting recipients and clicking the submit button

Figure 6

[icon: hand pointing right] Users can edit the Fleet Sharing record until it has been submitted.
[icon: hand pointing right] Once submitted, no further changes can be made, and the information is shared with the selected vessel(s).

---

[3] (Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — Step 2 – Discussion Record (p.5))
* <u>Enter the required Discussion Date under **Step 2**.</u>

* <u>Click **Submit** to confirm that the Fleet Sharing record has been discussed onboard. (See Figure 3)</u>

[icon: hand pointing right] Once the record is submitted, the **Confirmation** column in the Fleet Sharing overview table is automatically updated for both **Vessel** and **Office** users, indicating that the discussion has been completed.

---

[4] (Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — 1.2 FLEET SHARING – OVERVIEW (p.4))
The **Fleet Sharing** sub-module provides a consolidated view of Fleet Sharing records received from Office. The information displayed in this sub-module is automatically synchronized with the **Vessel Fleet Sharing** form whenever updates are made.

* <u>Use the **Filters** option to refine the displayed records.</u>

* <u>Click **Apply** to filter the records or click **Clear** to reset the filters.</u>

* <u>Use the **Actions** column to view or edit Fleet Sharing records. (See Figure 2).</u>

---

[5] (Audit - Fleet Sharing Manual- Vessel_R1_30.06.2026 — Preamble (p.6))
screenshot: Fleet Sharing interface showing Description and Discussion Record sections with callouts explaining the UI elements.

<u>Figure 3</u>

FULL ANSWER:
No. Step 1 information entered by the Office is shared for reference and is read-only. Vessel users cannot modify it.  

Source: Fleet Sharing Manual – Vessel, “Step 1 – Description,” p. 5.

* <u>Click **Apply** to filter the records or click **Clear** to reset the filters.</u>

* Click the 'Work Orders' sub-module to view all the work orders. (Ref Figure 32)

* Click the required status tab (Scheduled, Due, Overdue, Postponed, Unplanned, Pending Approval, and Completed) to view the corresponding work orders' status. (Ref Figure 32)

screenshot: Work Orders (W\.O) interface showing navigation and status tabs with instructional callouts.

<a id="figure-32"></a>Figure 32

* [icon: hand pointing right] **Scheduled:** Jobs that have been planned and have a future due date.
* **Due:** Jobs that are due within the current period.
* **Overdue:** Jobs past their due date that have not been completed.
* **Postponed:** Jobs that have been deferred with an approved justification.
* **Unplanned:** Details of unplanned work orders will be displayed in this section.
* **Pending Approval:** All submitted work order details will be displayed here for HOD approval.
* **Completed:** Fully executed and approved 'Work Order' details will be displayed in this tab.

Figure 77

FULL ANSWER:
No. The ROB cannot be modified unless a storage location is selected.

Supported ways to update it are:

1. **Inventory transaction**
   1. Select a location, or create one using **+ Create New Location**.
   2. Update the ROB.
   3. Click **Save**.
   4. Consumption cannot exceed the current ROB; updates are recorded in the **History** tab. This applies to both Ship and Office procedures.

2. **Update by location**
   1. Open the **Location** tab.
   2. Select a location from the **Location tree**.
   3. Update the **Loc ROB** value.
   4. Click **Save**. This procedure is documented for both Ship and Office.

**Source:**  
- Inventory transaction: *Technical - PMS User Manual_Vessel Specific*, §1.1.7.2, p.38; *Technical - PMS User Manual For Office_Sail Admin*, p.45.  
- Update by location: *Technical - PMS User Manual_Vessel Specific*, §1.1.7.3, p.39; *Technical - PMS User Manual For Office_Sail Admin*, §1.1.7.4, p.45.

FULL ANSWER:
No. A storage location must be selected before updating a spare part’s ROB.

1. **Inventory transaction update:** Select a location—or create one with **+ Create New Location**—then update and save. The ROB cannot be modified unless a location is selected. Consumption cannot exceed the current ROB, and updates are recorded in **History**. This applies to both Ship and Office procedures.

2. **Update spares by location:** Open the **Location** tab, select a location from the **Location tree**, update the **Loc ROB**, and click **Save**.

**Source:**
- Inventory transaction update — Vessel Specific manual, §1.1.7.2, p.38; Office manual, p.45.
- Update spares by location — Vessel Specific manual, §1.1.7.3, p.39; Office manual, §1.1.7.4, p.45.

FULL ANSWER:
No. A spare part’s ROB cannot be modified unless a storage location is selected first.

Supported ways to update it are:

1. **Inventory transaction screen — Vessel and Office**
   - Select an existing location, or click **+ Create New Location**.
   - Update the ROB field.
   - Click **Save**.
   - ROB updates are recorded in the **History** tab.

2. **Spares By Location — Vessel and Office**
   - Open the **Location** tab.
   - Select a location from the **Location tree**.
   - Click the **Loc ROB** value in the table.
   - Update it and click **Save**.

**Source:**  
- Inventory transaction: *Technical - PMS User Manual_Vessel Specific*, §1.1.7.2, p.38; *Technical - PMS User Manual For Office_Sail Admin*, p.45.  
- Spares By Location: *Technical - PMS User Manual_Vessel Specific*, §1.1.7.3, p.39; *Technical - PMS User Manual For Office_Sail Admin*, §1.1.7.4, p.45.

FULL ANSWER:
No. A spare part’s ROB cannot be modified until a storage location is selected.

1. **Inventory transaction:** Select a location, or create a new one with **+ Create New Location**, then click **Save**. Consumption cannot exceed the current ROB, and updates are recorded in the **History** tab. This applies to vessel and office inventory screens.
2. **By location:** Open the **Location** tab, select a location from the **Location tree**, update the **Loc ROB**, and click **Save**. This procedure is documented for both vessel and office use.

**Source:**  
- Inventory transactions — *Technical PMS User Manual Vessel Specific*, §§1.1.7.2 and 1.1.8.5, p.38/44; *Technical PMS User Manual For Office*, p.45.  
- Update spares by location — *Technical PMS User Manual Vessel Specific*, §1.1.7.3, p.39; *Technical PMS User Manual For Office*, §1.1.7.4, p.45.

QUESTION: Does a crew promotion review form show up directly in the Promotion sub-module, or does something else have to happen first?
JUDGING RULE: must ['admin', 'released']; must_not ['automatically available', 'promotion sub-module directly']; expected Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026.pdf p[48]. Evidence: Promotion forms are created and released through the 'Admin' sub-module before they become available in the Promotion sub-module.

---

[2] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.6.1.5 HOW TO EXECUTE A PROMOTION (p.48))
* Click on the 'Execution' section from the left navigation panel. (Ref Figure 57)

* Select all required details in Part C and click on the 'Save' button to store it as a draft and click on the 'Submit' button to finalize and complete the promotion execution process. (Ref Figure 57)

screenshot_from_computer: Promotion Review Form showing Part C - Execution section with callouts: 1. Click here to open the 'Part C' section and enter the required details. 2. Click here to save the form or submit the form.

<u>Figure 57</u>

[icon: hand pointing right] Promotion forms are created and released through the 'Admin' sub—module before they become available in the Promotion sub-module.

---

[3] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — Introduction (p.45))
* The Crew Promotion module provides a centralized view of crew members who are eligible or being considered for promotion. It enables users to monitor promotion readiness, review qualification requirements, track checklist progress, and manage the approval workflow based on defined criteria such as licenses, age, sea service, training, recommendations, CES scores, and other competency requirements.

---

[4] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — Document (p.100))
* The screen below appears after clicking the 'Edit' icon. (Ref Figure 127)

* Enter and add all required information in 'Part A' and 'Part B' section before proceeding. (Ref Figure 127)

* Click the 'Save Form' button to save the Promotion Review Form configuration. (Ref Figure 127)

screenshot: Promotion Review Form - Form Builder interface showing configuration options for promotion criteria and checklist

Figure 127

<mark>icon: pointing hand After the form is saved, promotion form can be filled from the 'Promotion' sub-module.</mark>

---

[5] (Crewing - Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026 — 1.6.1.1 HOW TO ACCESS PROMOTIONS (p.45))
* Click on the ‘Promotions’ submodule, the system automatically navigates to the ‘All’ sub-submodule as the default view. <u>(Ref Figure 50)</u>

screenshot_from_computer: Crew Promotion dashboard showing the Promotions submodule selected. An annotation box points to the Promotions icon and states: 1. Click the 'Promotions' sub-module to open the 'All' sub-submodule by default module.

<a id="figure-50"></a>Figure 50

FULL ANSWER:
It does **not** appear directly by default. The promotion form must first be created and released through the **Admin** sub-module:

1. In Admin, open the form configuration.
2. Enter all required information in **Part A** and **Part B**.
3. Click **Save Form**.
4. The promotion form can then be filled in from the **Promotion** sub-module.

Afterward, in Promotion, select the crew member and click **Edit** to open the promotion review form.

**Source:**
- Admin form configuration — *Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026*, “Document,” p.100.
- Form release and review access — *Crewing User Manual R2_10.06.2026*, “How to Execute a Promotion,” p.48; “How to Review a Promotion Record,” p.46.

[2] (Technical - PMS User Manual_Vessel Specific_R3_08.07.2026 — Introduction (p.47))
* The Modify PMS module provides a governed change-management process for modifying the PMS structure. Any change to a component record, maintenance job, spare part specification, or stores item must be submitted as a formal Change Request (CR) and approved before it takes effect. No. As a **Vessel User**, the Modify PMS Change Request option is not available. The Modify PMS interface for managing change requests is displayed only to the **HOD**.

The documented creation flow—click **“+ New Change Request”**, then select **Components**—therefore does not provide a Vessel User with a self-service option.

**Source:** Vessel PMS User Manual, **1.1.10.1 How to Access Modify PMS**, p. 47; **1.1.10.2 How to Create a Change Request for Components**, p. 48.

FULL ANSWER:
No. As a **Vessel User**, you cannot create a new Modify PMS change request yourself. The Change Request screen is available only to the **HOD**; for Vessel Users, the Change Request option is not available.

**Source:** Vessel PMS User Manual, §1.1.10.1 “How to Access Modify PMS,” p.47.

FULL ANSWER:
No. As a **Vessel User**, the Modify PMS Change Request option is not available. The Change Requests screen is displayed only to the **HOD**, so a Vessel User cannot create a new request themselves.

The documented creation steps for an authorized user are to click **“+ New Change Request”** and select the **“Components”** card for a component change.

**Source:**  
- Vessel PMS User Manual, **1.1.10.1 How to Access Modify PMS**, p. 47  
- Vessel PMS User Manual, **1.1.10.2 How to Create a Change Request for Components**, p. 48
